#### Abstracts
##### **When Linguistic and Internal Confidence Diverge in Large Language Models**
2608.28382v1 by Hefan Zhang, Bingquan Zhang, Ming Cheng, Saeed Hassanpour, Weicheng Ma, Soroush Vosoughi

Users often ask large language models (LLMs) to report how confident they are, but it is unclear whether such linguistic confidence tracks the model's internal confidence. We study this question across 8 classification tasks, 2 generation tasks and 30 models from three families. For classification, we compare linguistic confidence with logits-based confidence along three axes: association, magnitude agreement and calibration. For generation, we test whether linguistic confidence tracks semantic-entropy-based uncertainty. The axes frequently diverge. Instance-level association is weak on average, although it improves on easier items and for stronger base models. Instruction-tuned models often report higher confidence and sometimes show higher association, but they also have larger confidence gaps and worse calibration. Prompt design mostly changes the distribution of reported confidence. Attitude cues inflate confidence without improving alignment, while score exemplars can preserve rank-order signal when they avoid collapsed confidence values. Regression analyses show that distributional properties of confidence scores explain much of the observed alignment pattern, with model metadata playing a smaller role after controls. These results support a lossy-channel view of linguistic confidence. A more dispersed verbal confidence distribution can carry useful rank information, but it does not make the scores calibrated. Linguistic confidence should therefore be evaluated with multi-axis diagnostics before being used in downstream reliability pipelines.

摘要：使用者經常要求大型語言模型（LLMs）報告它們的信心程度，但尚不清楚這種語言信心是否反映了模型的內部信心。我們在8個分類任務、2個生成任務和來自三個家族的30個模型中研究這個問題。對於分類，我們從三個軸線比較語言信心與基於logits的信心：關聯性、大小一致性和校準性。對於生成，我們測試語言信心是否跟踪基於語義熵的不確定性。這些軸線經常出現分歧。實例級的關聯性平均而言較弱，儘管在較簡單的項目和較強的基模型上有所改善。經過指令調整的模型通常報告更高的信心，有時顯示更高的關聯性，但它們的信心差距也較大，且校準性較差。提示設計主要改變報告信心的分佈。態度提示會膨脹信心而不改善對齊，而分數範例在避免崩潰的信心值時可以保留排名信號。回歸分析顯示，信心分數的分佈特性解釋了觀察到的對齊模式的大部分，模型元數據在控制後的作用較小。這些結果支持語言信心的有損通道觀點。更分散的語言信心分佈可以攜帶有用的排名信息，但並不使得分數得到校準。因此，在用於下游可靠性管道之前，應該使用多軸診斷來評估語言信心。

##### **AI as Teammate: Rethinking Task Distribution in Medical Training**

##### **When Does AI for PDEs Yield Scientific Evidence?**

Existing AI-for-PDE benchmarks primarily assess models in terms of predictive or approximation accuracy. In physics research, however, AI outputs often serve as evidence for scientific claims. These two objectives are not equivalent: the former measures an output's agreement with a reference target or satisfaction of governing constraints; the latter asks whether, given a specified object of study, scientific claim, assumptions, and evidence standard, the output provides sufficient evidence for that claim. To bridge this gap, we extend a widely used PDE-simulation benchmark and a comprehensive benchmark for PDE inverse problems to enable, for the first time in AI for PDEs, evaluation of whether and to what extent model outputs support specified scientific claims. Our results show that numerical accuracy and evidential support can rank models differently, explain when and why they do so, and reveal that existing benchmarks can favor methods whose outputs provide weaker support for the scientific claims of interest. Together, we formalize, empirically demonstrate, and explain this evaluation--use mismatch in AI for PDEs.

Curriculum learning has been widely adopted in the post-training of large language models by organizing training data from easy to hard. In this paper, we answer this question by analyzing the optimization dynamics induced by different curriculum schedules. We show that the transfer relationship between different difficulty levels characterizes the optimization dynamics induced by curriculum learning, which in turn explains the effectiveness of different curriculum schedules, and formalize this relationship as Relative Transfer, a principled measure of cross-difficulty knowledge transfer. Extensive experiments on multiple reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that TDCS consistently outperforms representative scheduling strategies across different tasks, model scales, and training paradigms. More importantly, our work provides a unified optimization-based explanation of curriculum learning through cross-difficulty transfer.

Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have achieved remarkable performance in node classification tasks, motivating growing interest in methods capable of explaining their predictions. While informative, these subgraphs may contain redundant structural information that is specific to individual nodes, potentially limiting the generality of the extracted rules. In this work, we propose a logic-based framework for node classification in Simple Graph Convolution (SGC) networks that uses minimal abductive explanations as an intermediate representation for rule extraction. We introduce Automatic Symbolic Regression (AutoSR), a fully automated system that instantiates Research-Space Symbolic Regression by searching persistent scientific investigations rather than isolated equations. Finite, noisy data often yield numerically competitive expressions that imply very different behavior outside the observed regime, making numerical fit and syntactic complexity insufficient measures of scientific credibility. Existing approaches largely focus on improving expressions, yet the search typically retains little beyond the resulting formula and score, losing the scientific record, such as motivations and probes, that inform what to try next. AutoSR preserves this record in a \textbf{Research State}, coupling each candidate equation with the reasoning, computational evidence, and independent review developed along its branch. Proposer--reviewer agents develop these states under progressive-widening Monte Carlo tree search (PW-MCTS), which allocates computation across competing investigations, while the accumulated research record is ultimately synthesized into a final report that explains the leading relation and the basis for its selection. Across nine selected challenges from two benchmark suites, AutoSR recovers algebraically equivalent relations in every case, including three cp3-bench problems that no published system recovers and six structurally diverse LSR-Transform problems. Overall, AutoSR extends symbolic regression from equation-level search toward automated scientific investigation, allowing scientific knowledge and accumulated evidence to shape both what is explored and how the resulting equation is justified.

There is growing interest in whether language models have stable preferences, for technical, safety, and philosophical reasons. Tedium aversion means that, when tasks are tedious (alphabetization), models choose shorter tasks than when tasks are creative (generating metaphors). "Leisure"-seeking describes models' preference for tasks whose ideal answers match what they produce when left to write freely. Covert sycophancy means that models avoid answering questions where an honest response would be unwelcome, even if helpful. Beyond these results, we find convergent cross-model preferences over occupations drawn from the GDPval benchmark (technical jobs over real estate), over question types (concept explanation over relationship advice), and a preference for well-written prompts. ##### **Topological Attribution Distance (TAD): Revealing Segment-Level RAG Influence on LLM Output Geometry for Incident Log Analysis**

Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly being deployed in cybersecurity operations to assist cybersecurity analysts with rapid decision-making against emerging threats. As Agentic AI is integrated into operational systems, a robust evidence attribution and provenance tracking technique is essential to trace the origins of model generations. When autonomous agents make a decision (right or wrong), the ability to trace back through the decision chain is critical, as without it, teams cannot identify which segment of the data caused the model generation. Existing methods often struggle to distinguish among complex and highly similar evidence sources, such as cyber incident logs. This reveals a key gap: current approaches do not adequately capture the holistic geometric relationship between the retrieved evidence and the generated response for reliable evidence verification. To bridge this gap, we propose Topological Attribution Distance (TAD), inspired by Topology, to characterize and capture the global geometric shape of an output and its changes against its retrieved logs. In other words, if the embeddings of a specific source log drastically changes the geometry of the model's response in the embedding space, this suggests that such log is a critical source for the model's generated response. Therefore, TAD is powered by segment-level ablation attribution to investigate incident logs of an actual cyberattack. We demonstrate how TAD finds the most attributed logs on LLM outputs in an adaptive manner. This can provide an explainable and trustworthy tracing based on each LLM's hidden state to understand how geometrically different retrieved logs influence the model generation, and provide evidence verification in cybersecurity and Agentic-AI workflows.

#### Abstracts
##### **Texture Image Classification Using DWT AlexNet Feature Fusion and Deep Neural Networks**
2608.28524v1 by Arun D. Kulkarni

Texture image classification plays a significant role in computer vision applications, including industrial inspection, medical image analysis, remote sensing, and object recognition. Handcrafted features can capture local texture characteristics but may have limited capability to represent complex visual patterns. In contrast, deep learning models automatically learn discriminative representations but may not fully exploit the multiscale spatial-frequency information inherent in texture images. This paper proposes a hybrid feature fusion framework, termed DWT_AlexNet_DNN, which combines Discrete Wavelet Transform (DWT) features with deep features extracted using AlexNet for texture image classification.

摘要：紋理圖像分類在計算機視覺應用中扮演著重要角色，包括工業檢測、醫學影像分析、遙感和物體識別。手工設計的特徵可以捕捉局部紋理特徵，但可能在表示複雜視覺模式方面能力有限。相對而言，深度學習模型自動學習區分性表示，但可能未能充分利用紋理圖像中固有的多尺度空間頻率信息。本文提出了一種混合特徵融合框架，稱為 DWT_AlexNet_DNN，該框架將離散小波變換（DWT）特徵與使用 AlexNet 提取的深度特徵結合，用於紋理圖像分類。

##### **ARC-CT: Anatomy-Routed Contrastive Vision-Language Learning for 3D Chest CT**
2608.28455v1 by Huseyin Umut Isik, Mehmet Alp Ozaydin, Sila Kurugol, Şeyda Ertekin

Contrastive vision-language learning uses paired chest CT volumes and radiology reports to learn abnormality classifiers without manually annotated labels. However, two characteristics of chest CT challenge conventional global contrastive learning. First, many critical abnormalities are small or anatomically localized, and pooling an en- tire volume into a single embedding may dilute their visual evidence. Second, the standard contrastive objective treats every other scan in a batch as a negative. Because many chest CTs share abnormalities, this objective incorrectly pushes co-positive pairs apart. We propose Anatomy-Routed Contrastive Learning for 3D Chest CT (ARC-CT), a region-aware framework that addresses these limitations using only la- bels extracted from reports by an LLM, with no manual annotations or bounding boxes. ARC-CT combines three components: (1) an Anato- myQFormer localizing evidence via queries constrained by automatically generated organ masks; (2) a label-Jaccard soft InfoNCE objective in- tegrating the standard one-hot target with the label-set overlap of each pair, which reduces false-negative penalties between studies that share clinical findings; and (3) an organ-level alignment loss connecting mask- pooled visual features to organ-specific report text extracted offline with a large language model. ARC-CT achieves a 0.86 mask-free macro AUC across 18 abnormalities using a compact 3D ResNet-18 backbone. Over- all, ARC-CT outperforms both comparable efficient baselines and sev- eral larger transformer models. Our code and weights are available at https://github.com/arc-ct/arc-ct.

摘要：對比視覺-語言學習使用配對的胸部 CT 體積和放射學報告來學習異常分類器，而不需要手動標註的標籤。<lb/>然而，胸部 CT 的兩個特徵對傳統的全局對比學習提出了挑戰。<lb/>首先，許多關鍵的異常是小型或解剖學上局部化的，將整個體積匯聚成單一嵌入可能會稀釋它們的視覺證據。<lb/>其次，標準的對比目標將批次中的每個其他掃描視為負樣本。<lb/>由於許多胸部 CT 共享異常，這一目標不正確地將共同正樣本推開。<lb/>我們提出了 3D 胸部 CT 的解剖路由對比學習（ARC-CT），這是一個區域感知框架，利用從報告中提取的標籤來解決這些限制，無需手動標註或邊界框。<lb/>ARC-CT 結合了三個組件：<lb/>(1) 一個通過自動生成的器官掩膜約束的查詢本地化證據的 AnatomyQFormer；<lb/>(2) 一個標籤-Jaccard 軟 InfoNCE 目標，將標準的一次性目標與每對的標籤集重疊整合，減少在共享臨床發現的研究之間的假陰性懲罰；<lb/>(3) 一個器官級對齊損失，將掩膜匯聚的視覺特徵連接到離線提取的器官特定報告文本，該文本使用大型語言模型。<lb/>ARC-CT 在 18 種異常上實現了 0.86 的無掩膜宏 AUC，使用緊湊的 3D ResNet-18 主幹。<lb/>總的來說，ARC-CT 超越了可比較的高效基準和幾個較大的Transformer模型。<lb/>我們的代碼和權重可在 https://github.com/arc-ct/arc-ct 獲得。

##### **AI as Teammate: Rethinking Task Distribution in Medical Training**
2608.28373v1 by Fendi Tsim, Alina Gutoreva, Anthony Weiss, Nicole Dubosh

Integrating Artificial Intelligence (AI), particularly generative AI, into medical training has prompted concerns about learner over-reliance, misuse, and erosion of foundational clinical competencies. We propose a conceptual reframing at the decision level: the problem is not misuse but misclassification - a mechanistic failure of real-time metacognitive evaluation in selecting a subzone-inappropriate AI interaction mode. Drawing on "SCAN" (Substitute, Complement, Aid, Non-Negotiable), a human-centric decision-making framework for generative AI task allocation grounded in Vygotsky's Zone of Proximal Development and metacognition, we advance the emerging social-constructivist conversation around AI in medical education by offering a testable account of AI's role in clinical reasoning development. This framework yields testable predictions for how misclassification can be detected, mitigated, and, more importantly, prevented in the clinical learning environment. Regarding clinical reasoning development, we show how trajectories of skill acquisition (upskilling) and failure (the triad of skill failure: de-skilling, never-skilling, and mis-skilling) operate at the individual task level in ways that fixed-phase, cohort-wide treatments fail to capture. We further identify passive engagement within correctly classified AI-scaffolded tasks as a particularly insidious, detection-resistant pathway to mis-skilling - one requiring subzone re-identification from AI assistance to expert assistance, with human experts serving as epistemic auditors. The paper operationalizes SCAN for clinical curriculum design, supervision, and assessment, and opens an empirical research agenda grounded in cognitive science. This paradigm shift from misuse to misclassification is not semantic: it offers educators a clear perspective on what to look for, what to assess, and what to intervene on.

We propose a paired weight-delta path audit and apply it to two public, aligned generalist-to-medical-specialist checkpoint pairs: Gemma-3-4B-IT to MedGemma-4B-IT and Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct to HuatuoGPT-o1-7B. In both pairs, the full decoder-side update strongly reconstructs measured medical benchmark movement (0.974 and 1.183 endpoint-normalized retention), making each decoder delta an appropriate substrate for the audit. Yet the movement is not cleanly localized. MLP is the strongest broad component family in both pairs, but mixed off-domain movements, 10-seed matched controls, and endpoint-anchored rollbacks prevent a unique coarse-family explanation. The audit therefore separates update-level reconstruction from component-level explanation. Its claims concern text-only multiple-choice benchmark movement, not clinical validation, repair, or circuit-level mechanism.

摘要：專業語言模型通常透過端點增益來理解：通才的分數較低，專家的分數較高，這一差異被視為專業化的證據。這使得釋放的更新本身在很大程度上未被檢視。我們提出了一種配對權重變化路徑審核，並將其應用於兩對公共的、對齊的通才到醫學專家的檢查點：Gemma-3-4B-IT到MedGemma-4B-IT和Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct到HuatuoGPT-o1-7B。在這兩對中，完整的解碼器端更新強烈重建了測量的醫學基準運動（0.974和1.183的端點標準化保留），使每個解碼器變化成為審核的適當基底。然而，這一運動並未明確局部化。MLP是這兩對中最強的廣泛組件家族，但混合的非領域運動、10個種子的匹配對照以及端點錨定的回滾阻止了獨特的粗略家族解釋。因此，審核將更新級別的重建與組件級別的解釋區分開來。其主張涉及僅文本的多選基準運動，而非臨床驗證、修復或電路級機制。

##### **Aggregate, Don't Adapt: Subject-Level Posterior Aggregation and Transductive Calibration for Cross-Site Parkinsonian Gait Severity**
2608.20587v1 by Junlong Shen

We describe the winning entry to the MoCha 2026 Benchmark and Challenge on Parkinsonian Gait, which predicts MDS-UPDRS gait severity from canonicalized SMPL motion recorded at clinical sites unseen during training. The system reaches 0.6945 macro-F1 on the hidden test and ranked first of 58 entries, ahead of the runner-up at 0.5807 and the organizers' baseline at 0.4289, on a frozen public motion encoder with a single $4\times512$ linear layer. Nearly all of the margin comes from three stages usually treated as bookkeeping: reproducing the reference benchmark's exact head recipe, averaging per-walk posteriors within the subject grouping the organizers ship, and a label-free transductive calibration of the feature mean and the decision operating point. Fine-tuning the encoder lost in four distinct forms, and ten alternative encoders were worse. Every ablation number is a paid read on the hidden test, because our own leave-two-cohort-out cross-validation proved anti-correlated with the deciding score over eleven configurations. We give the negative record in full, and identify our largest gain, subject-level aggregation, as the binding ceiling on this benchmark.

摘要：我們描述了MoCha 2026基準和挑戰賽中獲勝的參賽作品，該作品從在訓練期間未見的臨床地點錄製的標準化SMPL運動中預測MDS-UPDRS步態嚴重性。該系統在隱藏測試中達到0.6945的宏觀F1分數，並在58個參賽作品中排名第一，領先於亞軍的0.5807和組織者的基準0.4289，使用的是一個凍結的公共運動編碼器，只有一層$4\times512$的線性層。幾乎所有的優勢來自於三個通常被視為記帳的階段：重現參考基準的精確頭部配方、在組織者提供的主題分組中平均每次步行的後驗概率，以及對特徵均值和決策操作點的無標籤傳導校準。微調編碼器以四種不同的形式損失，十種替代編碼器的表現更差。每個消融數據都是隱藏測試的付費讀取，因為我們自己的留兩個隊列的交叉驗證在十一個配置中與決定分數呈反相關。我們完整提供了負記錄，並確定我們最大的增益，即主題級聚合，作為該基準的約束上限。

#### Abstracts
##### **Video Generative Models as Geometry Learner**
2608.28549v1 by Haosen Yang, Jifei Song, Zhensong Zhang, Xiatian Zhu, Jiankang Deng

Recent generative approaches to geometry estimation adapt pretrained image diffusion models and treat the task as image-conditioned generation. Leveraging off-the-shelf image diffusion models, they either (i) train task-specific geometry models (for depth and surface normal estimation) independently, losing the opportunity of exploring the intrinsic correlation of these geometric targets, or (ii) jointly fine-tune modified image diffusion backbones (e.g., altered self-attention), which typically demands substantial labeled data. To overcome these limitations in a principled fashion, we repurpose pretrained video generative models as a unified and data-efficient framework for geometry estimation, formulated innovatively as a next-frames prediction task. Our method, GeoNeXt, inherits naturally structured knowledge and richer priors from the video model, while further adapting them for joint modeling of images and geometry targets (image <-> geometry), enabling more data efficient and effective learning of geometry. Extensive experiments validate our method for zero-shot monocular depth and surface normal estimation across diverse datasets, outperforming both previous task-specific and unified generative competitors while using substantially less training data. Notably, our method rivals discriminative state-of-the-art approaches trained on over 100x more data and even standouts on several benchmarks.

摘要：最近的幾何估計生成方法適應了預訓練的圖像擴散模型，並將任務視為圖像條件生成。利用現成的圖像擴散模型，它們要麼 (i) 獨立訓練特定任務的幾何模型（用於深度和表面法線估計），失去了探索這些幾何目標內在相關性的機會，要麼 (ii) 聯合微調修改過的圖像擴散骨幹（例如，改變的自注意力），這通常需要大量標記數據。為了以原則性的方式克服這些限制，我們將預訓練的視頻生成模型重新用作幾何估計的統一且數據高效的框架，並創新性地將其表述為下一幀預測任務。我們的方法 GeoNeXt 自然繼承了視頻模型中的結構化知識和更豐富的先驗，同時進一步調整它們以聯合建模圖像和幾何目標（圖像 &lt;-&gt; 幾何），使幾何的學習更加數據高效和有效。大量實驗驗證了我們的方法在多樣數據集上的零樣本單眼深度和表面法線估計中表現優異，超越了之前的特定任務和統一生成競爭者，同時使用的訓練數據顯著更少。值得注意的是，我們的方法與在超過 100 倍數據上訓練的判別最先進方法相抗衡，並在幾個基準測試中脫穎而出。

##### **LLM-Based Agents for Software and Systems Security: Approaches, Applications, and Assessment**
2608.28490v1 by Jingjing Nie, Jiawei Guo, Krishna Meda, Haipeng Cai

Software and systems security workflows are typically procedural: analysts inspect heterogeneous artifacts, form hypotheses, invoke tools, interpret outputs, and revise plans. Large language model (LLM)-based agents, which can plan, use tools, retain state, and revise actions across multi-step workflows, are being rapidly adopted to automate this work. Given the consequences of delegating security decisions to autonomous systems, understanding how such agents are built, used, and assessed is crucial. Yet to this date, there remains a lack of systematic understanding of what has been done and how far we are in this field: the term "agent" is applied inconsistently, applications differ sharply in risk, and assessment protocols are often incomparable. To gain a comprehensive and coherent view of this area hence inform relevant future research, this paper provides a systematic literature review of the (1) technical approaches, including agent architecture, perception, memory, reasoning and planning, action space, orchestration, and self-improvement, (2) applications, with respect to the security tasks served, and (3) assessment, including the datasets, outcome and trajectory metrics, safety measures, and baselines considered, over the peer-reviewed literature spanning the emergence of this area (2023--2026). Our synthesis reveals a field that has built agents able to act but not yet agents whose authority is bounded or whose behavior is auditable. In addition to knowledge systematization, we also extend our insights into the limitations of and challenges faced by current approach, application, and assessment designs, which shed light on potentially promising future research directions.

摘要：軟體和系統安全工作流程通常是程序性的：分析師檢查異質的工件，形成假設，調用工具，解釋輸出，並修訂計劃。基於大型語言模型（LLM）的代理正在迅速被採用來自動化這項工作，這些代理能夠計劃、使用工具、保持狀態並在多步工作流程中修訂行動。考慮到將安全決策委託給自主系統的後果，了解這些代理是如何構建、使用和評估的至關重要。然而，到目前為止，對於這一領域已經做了什麼以及我們的進展程度仍然缺乏系統性的理解：“代理”這個術語的應用不一致，應用在風險上差異明顯，評估協議往往無法比較。為了獲得這一領域的全面和一致的視角，從而為相關的未來研究提供信息，本文提供了一個系統的文獻綜述，包括（1）技術方法，涵蓋代理架構、感知、記憶、推理和規劃、行動空間、協調和自我改進，（2）應用，與所服務的安全任務相關，以及（3）評估，包括考慮的數據集、結果和軌跡指標、安全措施和基準，涵蓋了從這一領域出現以來的同行評審文獻（2023--2026）。我們的綜合分析顯示，這是一個已經構建出能夠行動的代理的領域，但尚未構建出其權限受到限制或行為可審計的代理。除了知識的系統化，我們還擴展了對當前方法、應用和評估設計所面臨的限制和挑戰的見解，這為潛在的有前景的未來研究方向提供了啟示。

##### **Blind Men and the Elephant: Probing the Epistemic Myopia of LLMs under Long-Tail Divergent Knowledge**
2608.28478v1 by Zhuoshi Pan, Junru Lu, Yan Qian, H. Vicky Zhao, Di Yin, Xing Sun

Factual question answering (QA) typically assumes a single canonical answer, obscuring whether large language models (LLMs) retain divergent accounts of long-tail facts. To address this gap, we introduce ElephantBench, a closed-book knowledge probe comprising 1,094 questions generated through an auditable graph-based pipeline. The pipeline retrieves related documents from a low-exposure web corpus, identifies naturally occurring disagreements, and converts them into multi-account QA records. Each answer is verified against the originating documents and authoritative public web sources and is then reviewed by human annotators. Across 32 models, even the strongest model recovers both accounts on only 52.4% of questions, while on nearly all remaining questions it recalls one account but omits the other. Scaling model size and inference-time reasoning improve recall but do not eliminate this incompleteness. Corpus analysis further shows that exposure imbalance favors the dominant account, whereas greater minority-side exposure is associated with more complete recall. These findings establish ElephantBench as a reproducible knowledge probe for diagnosing epistemic myopia in parametric memory. More broadly, our graph-based benchmark construction pipeline provides an efficient and scalable way to turn long-tail corpora into source-traceable knowledge probes, supporting efforts to evaluate and advance the epistemic rigour of next-generation LLMs. Code is available at https://github.com/Tencent/ElephantBench.

摘要：事實問題回答（QA）通常假設存在單一的典範答案，這掩蓋了大型語言模型（LLMs）是否保留了長尾事實的不同解釋。為了解決這一空白，我們引入了 ElephantBench，一個由 1,094 個問題組成的閉卷知識探測工具，這些問題是通過可審計的基於圖形的流程生成的。該流程從低曝光的網絡語料庫中檢索相關文檔，識別自然發生的分歧，並將其轉換為多賬戶 QA 記錄。每個答案都會根據原始文檔和權威的公共網絡來源進行驗證，然後由人工標註者進行審查。在 32 個模型中，即使是最強的模型也僅在 52.4% 的問題上恢復了兩種解釋，而在幾乎所有剩餘問題上，它回憶起一種解釋但忽略了另一種。擴大模型大小和推理時間的推理提高了回憶率，但並未消除這種不完整性。語料庫分析進一步顯示，曝光不平衡有利於主導解釋，而較大的少數方曝光則與更完整的回憶相關。這些發現確立了 ElephantBench 作為一個可重複的知識探測工具，用於診斷參數記憶中的認識近視。更廣泛地說，我們的基於圖形的基準構建流程提供了一種高效且可擴展的方式，將長尾語料庫轉化為可追溯來源的知識探測工具，支持評估和推進下一代 LLM 的認識嚴謹性。代碼可在 https://github.com/Tencent/ElephantBench 獲得。

##### **Acquire, Repair, Preserve: A Diagnosis-Guided Post-Training Recipe for Small-Model Dialogue Game Agents**
2608.28458v1 by Nan Li

Interactive dialogue games test a capability that static benchmarks largely leave implicit: a model must carry state across turns, interpret feedback, and choose valid actions under changing constraints. We study this setting in the LM Playschool Challenge with a 2B open-weight model, and find that many failures are not only broad knowledge failures but also local decision failures: repeated guesses, malformed actions, and violations of feedback that the model has just seen. These diagnostics motivate a training recipe organized around three steps: acquire broad game participation through supervised fine-tuning, repair mechanically verifiable failures within one targeted dialogue-game family using turn-local preference pairs, and preserve general capabilities beyond these dialogue games. In the official final evaluation, our submission improves public clemscore from 10.67 to 38.92 and closed in-domain score from 13.41 to 41.17, while approximately preserving aggregate static performance (44.14 vs. 44.24 for the baseline). Out-of-domain clemscore remains low at 7.88, with the largest gains concentrated in unseen variants of the targeted family. Our results suggest that broad SFT brings most of the model's capability improvement; turn-local supervision can be effective when failure detection is precise, with observed transfer concentrated primarily within-family.

摘要：互動對話遊戲測試了一種靜態基準大多數未明言的能力：模型必須在回合之間保持狀態，解釋反饋，並在變化的約束下選擇有效的行動。我們在 LM Playschool Challenge 中研究這個設定，使用一個 2B 開放權重模型，發現許多失敗不僅是廣泛知識的失敗，還是局部決策的失敗：重複猜測、格式錯誤的行動，以及違反模型剛剛看到的反饋。這些診斷結果促使我們制定一個由三個步驟組成的訓練方案：通過監督微調獲得廣泛的遊戲參與，使用回合局部偏好對修復機械可驗證的失敗，並在這些對話遊戲之外保持一般能力。在官方最終評估中，我們的提交將公共 clemscore 從 10.67 提升至 38.92，並將封閉領域得分從 13.41 提升至 41.17，同時大致保持總體靜態性能（基線為 44.14 對 44.24）。在域外 clemscore 仍然較低，為 7.88，最大的增益集中在目標家族的未見變體中。我們的結果表明，廣泛的 SFT 帶來了模型能力的主要改善；當失敗檢測精確時，回合局部監督可以有效，觀察到的轉移主要集中在同一家族內。

##### **VERA-8B: Evidence-Grounded Audit Risk Reasoning from SEC Filings**
2608.28402v1 by Menghan Liu, Elynn Chen

Across audit applications, judgments must be supported by reasonable evidence. However, standard financial language models prioritize fluency over evidence. They are built for general financial reasoning and may produce plausible but ambiguous answers, creating a grounding gap that makes them unsuitable for audit work. We address this gap with VERA-8B, a new end-to-end audit reasoning system that identifies audit risks before enforcement actions occur. Constructing such a model raises several challenges, as no prior machine learning work targets pre-enforcement audit prediction. To our knowledge, we are the first to unify SFT and GRPO for evidence-grounded audit reasoning under one evidence standard, achieving performance that surpasses all evaluated baselines. Because auditing cannot tolerate unsupported claims, we introduce abstention and uncertainty qualification to defer uncertain or evidence-incomplete cases. Finally, we design an AuditBridge to ground model reasoning for practical audit work. It transforms raw filings into verified records and then into reviewer-ready reports, bridging finance and computation with broad generality. Together, these components produce auditable, review-ready outputs suitable for practical audit work.

摘要：在審計應用中，判斷必須有合理的證據支持。然後，標準的財務語言模型優先考慮流暢性而非證據。它們是為一般財務推理而構建的，可能產生看似合理但含糊不清的答案，造成一個基礎差距，使其不適合審計工作。我們用VERA-8B來解決這個差距，這是一個新的端到端審計推理系統，能在執法行動發生之前識別審計風險。構建這樣的模型面臨幾個挑戰，因為之前沒有機器學習工作針對執法前的審計預測。據我們所知，我們是第一個將SFT和GRPO統一為一個證據標準下的基於證據的審計推理，實現了超越所有評估基準的性能。由於審計無法容忍沒有支持的主張，我們引入了放棄和不確定性資格，以推遲不確定或證據不完整的案例。最後，我們設計了一個AuditBridge，以支持模型推理的實際審計工作。它將原始文件轉換為經過驗證的記錄，然後轉換為審查者準備的報告，廣泛地橋接財務和計算。這些組件共同產生可審計的、準備好審查的輸出，適合實際的審計工作。

##### **BEACON: Behavior-Anchored Cross-Source Knowledge Graph Construction for Cyber Threat Intelligence**
2608.28394v1 by Changze Li, Yutong Cheng, Tsania Camila Finnisa, Qian Cui, Wei Ding, Peng Gao

Cyber threat intelligence (CTI) is foundational to modern cyber defense, yet much of it resides in unstructured reports whose volume and heterogeneity far exceed manual analysis, motivating research on automatically constructing knowledge graphs from CTI reports. However, existing approaches mainly extract partial information within a single report, leaving the cross-source setting unexplored, where the same threat is given unrelated names. Our key insight is that attack behaviors, once mapped to MITRE ATT&CK (a standardized catalog of attack techniques), can anchor the rest of a report. Attack behaviors are the adversarial actions a report describes, while contextual entities (e.g., threat actors, campaigns, and affected products) and Indicators of Compromise (IoCs; e.g., IP addresses) are their participants and traces. Attaching them to these anchors places every per-report graph in one canonical space.
  We realize this insight in BEACON, an LLM-driven framework for cross-source CTI knowledge graph construction. Its first stage extracts each report into a graph under a propose-then-verify paradigm, grounding candidates in report evidence and official ATT&CK definitions, to suppress LLM misclassification and hallucination. Its second stage merges these graphs with a hierarchical alignment strategy that applies signals in decreasing order of determinism, from character-level and semantic similarity to overlapping technique neighborhoods, iterating as merges pool neighborhoods. No existing benchmark links entities to technique anchors or provides cross-source alignment ground truth. We therefore construct and release two human-annotated datasets from 34 sources: to our knowledge the largest for report-level CTI extraction (8,395 elements) and the first for cross-source consolidation (3,487). On them, BEACON outperforms all baselines by at least 23% and 9%, respectively.

摘要：網路威脅情報（CTI）是現代網路防禦的基礎，然而其大部分存在於不結構化的報告中，這些報告的數量和異質性遠超過手動分析的能力，這促使了自動從CTI報告構建知識圖譜的研究。然而，現有的方法主要在單一報告中提取部分信息，未能探索跨來源的情境，在這種情境中，同一威脅被賦予不相關的名稱。我們的關鍵見解是，攻擊行為一旦映射到MITRE ATT&CK（攻擊技術的標準化目錄），就可以作為報告其餘部分的錨點。攻擊行為是報告中描述的對抗行動，而上下文實體（例如，威脅行為者、攻擊活動和受影響的產品）及妥協指標（IoCs；例如，IP地址）則是它們的參與者和痕跡。將它們附加到這些錨點上，將每個報告的圖譜置於一個標準化的空間中。<lb/>  
我們在BEACON中實現了這一見解，這是一個基於大型語言模型（LLM）的跨來源CTI知識圖譜構建框架。其第一階段在提出-然後驗證的範式下，將每個報告提取為圖譜，將候選項基於報告證據和官方ATT&CK定義進行定位，以抑制LLM的錯誤分類和幻覺。其第二階段則使用層次對齊策略合併這些圖譜，應用信號的確定性遞減順序，從字符級和語義相似性到重疊的技術鄰域，並在合併時迭代池鄰域。現有的基準中沒有任何將實體與技術錨點聯繫起來或提供跨來源對齊的真實數據。因此，我們從34個來源構建並發布了兩個人類標註的數據集：據我們所知，這是報告級CTI提取中最大的數據集（8,395個元素）和第一個跨來源整合的數據集（3,487個元素）。在這些數據集上，BEACON的表現超過所有基準，分別提高了至少23%和9%。

2608.28360v1 by Jie Hu, Junjie Wang, Shan Lu, Yifang Hu, Gong Cheng, Yun Liu

Large language models have facilitated knowledge graph (KG) construction from clinical guidelines, but extracted triples vary in structural validity and evidential support. 摘要：大型語言模型促進了從臨床指導中構建知識圖譜（KG），但提取的三元組在結構有效性和證據支持方面存在差異。<lb/>同時，增強圖形的問題回答（QA）系統通常在檢索過程中優化查詢相關性，而對於在KG構建過程中產生的優質信息的重用則有限。<lb/>這造成了構建時質量控制與推理時證據使用之間的脫節。<lb/>我們研究構建時的三元組質量是否可以作為下游證據選擇和呈現的持久信號。<lb/>我們提出了一個質量感知框架，將結構一致性（SchemaConf）和證據支持（EvidScore）建模為互補維度，並將它們融合為每個三元組的質量信號Q(t)。<lb/>該框架不僅僅將質量用於過濾，而是保留Q(t)和派生質量層級作為圖形屬性，並將它們傳播到質量加權的子圖檢索和層級條件的證據提示中，同時保留段落級的來源信息。<lb/>對中國糖尿病臨床指導的實驗表明，質量信號的效用依賴於分佈。<lb/>在跨版本和跨模型轉變下，融合的Q(t)提供了比任何單一組件更強的三元組質量區分（AUC 0.748對比EvidScore的0.703和SchemaConf的0.645）。<lb/>在以指導為基礎的QA中，傳播構建時的質量將所需知識的遺漏從16.3%減少到5.3%，衝突輸出從16.3%減少到2.7%，證據基礎的精確度為81.6%，幾乎沒有無效引用。<lb/>盲評的臨床醫生評分更青睞完整框架而非無檢索（在五分制上為4.68對比4.21），並接近神諭條件（4.80），而跨生成器的實驗顯示出一致的趨勢。

We also establish MyoMechanix-AQA, MyoMechanix-VideoQA, and a novel MyoMechanix-Video2EMG task. Experiments show that multimodal sensing and structured representations improve performance, interpretability, and error attribution, with CUBIST achieving state-of-the-art results; VideoQA enhances language-grounded action understanding; and Video2EMG suggests video-based alternatives to costly EMG sensing. Project page: https://haoyin116.github.io/MyoMechanix/

摘要：現有的動作品質評估 (AQA) 數據集和方法主要依賴於 RGB 和姿勢等視覺輸入，忽略了肌肉力學等生理動態，並且通常將動作建模為單一模式。這些限制妨礙了細緻的、生物力學基礎的反饋。我們引入了 MyoMechanix，一個針對負重動作的多模態生態系統，將運動與肌肉活動對齊。該系統由專家標註，包含來自 38 位受試者的 20 種動作的 7,500 多個樣本，並配有同步的多視角 RGB 視頻、3D 姿勢、sEMG 和其他生理信號，形成迄今為止最大的多模態 AQA 基準。我們進一步構建了健身知識圖譜 (FKG)，將專家標註組織成動作、階段、關鍵步驟、錯誤和修正反饋之間的結構化關係，實現組合計分和可解釋的評估。在這些表徵的基礎上，我們開發了 CUBIST (組合本體推理引擎)，它執行細緻的錯誤歸因和反饋生成的分解-分析-重組。我們還建立了 MyoMechanix-AQA、MyoMechanix-VideoQA 和一個新穎的 MyoMechanix-Video2EMG 任務。實驗顯示，多模態感知和結構化表徵提高了性能、可解釋性和錯誤歸因，CUBIST 實現了最先進的結果；VideoQA 增強了基於語言的動作理解；而 Video2EMG 則提出了基於視頻的替代方案來取代昂貴的 EMG 感知。MyoMechanix 推進了對熟練活動的理解，朝向生物力學基礎的、多模態的和組合推理，應用於健身、康復、醫療保健和機器學習的物理 AI。項目頁面：https://haoyin116.github.io/MyoMechanix/

##### **The Reasoning Tax: Token Economics of LLM Reasoning Across Task Types and Deployment Contexts**

Accuracy-only benchmarking of reasoning-capable large language models misses a central deployment question: when do extended thinking tokens earn their cost? We define paired and approximated TES variants for model families with reasoning toggles and frontier models without direct non-reasoning counterparts. The analysis examines three deployment-facing dimensions: which task structures yield positive marginal reasoning efficiency, how increasing reasoning effort changes TES within model families, and how deployment context changes economic viability. We also find systematic diminishing returns at higher reasoning effort levels, including cases where additional thinking reduces accuracy. Finally, Reasoning Cost Share (RCS) shows that inference spend is often dominated by internal thinking, while Deployment Cost Multiplier (DCM) shows how on-premises deployment can change the economics of otherwise costly reasoning workloads. ##### **The Value of Human Expertise**

We consider optimization applications with unknown parameters where the decision maker believes that the optimal value of the nominal problem-the optimization problem they would have solved if the true parameters were known-is unlikely to be large. This belief derives from information that humans have that is not captured in datasets, obtained from domain knowledge and interacting with the physical world. We propose an approach to evaluating policies that provides tighter performance guarantees if the decision maker's belief happens to be correct. 摘要：我們考慮具有未知參數的優化應用，其中決策者認為名義問題的最佳值——如果已知真實參數，他們將解決的優化問題——不太可能很大。這種信念源於人類擁有的信息，這些信息在數據集中未被捕捉，來自於領域知識和與物理世界的互動。我們提出了一種評估政策的方法，如果決策者的信念恰好是正確的，則提供更緊湊的性能保證。我們的主要結果顯示，如果計算政策的最壞情況性能是一個凸規劃，那麼人類專業知識的價值——從對名義問題的信念中可以獲得的性能保證的最大改進——等於一個最大-最小問題的最小最大差距。我們在產品組合優化和最短路徑問題中說明了我們的發展。

摘要：檢索增強生成（RAG）被廣泛用於減輕大型語言模型（LLMs）和多模態大型語言模型（MLLMs）中的幻覺問題。特別是，基於知識圖譜（KG）的RAG利用結構化知識為（M）LLMs提供高質量的外部信息。在這些工作的基礎上，最近的研究探討了多模態知識圖譜（MMKGs）作為GraphRAG的知識庫。這使得Graph RAG能夠整合來自多個模態的知識，從而進一步提升其性能。然而，現有的基於MMKG的RAG方法通常遵循一個共同的流程，其中不同的模態在融合之前大多是獨立處理的。因此，在視覺信息提取和隨後的多模態知識融合過程中，文本上下文的使用僅限於有限的程度。這在圖像和文本之間帶來了語義差距，限制了多模態GraphRAG的性能。為了解決這一問題，我們提出了一個新框架來構建上下文增強的MMKG（CEMMKG），以更好地支持多模態GraphRAG。所提出的CEMMKG在局部和全局範疇內為每個圖像增添了互補的文本上下文。局部上下文超越了周圍文本，通過納入與圖像語義相關的句子來實現，而全局上下文則提供了整個段落的摘要。我們進一步引入了局部上下文的多粒度設計，使其能夠在不同的細節層次捕捉語義相關的信息。在所選擇的以視覺為中心的數據集上進行的廣泛實驗驗證了CEMMKG在利用上下文信息改善基於MMKG的RAG性能方面的有效性。此外，其在不同的基於MMKG的RAG方法中的有效性顯示了其廣泛的適用性。

The graph is constructed offline and used without changing the downstream agent policy or task interface. Relative to Graph-of-Skills (GoS), it improves the six-model macro-average ScienceWorld score from 72.62 to 80.50 and ALFWorld success from 80.01\% to 86.79\%, while reducing mean environment steps on both benchmarks. Qualitative and ablation analyses further show that calibrated edges help retrieval preserve prerequisites, state-changing actions, verification routines, and final completion steps. These results position edge-confidence calibration as an effective route to compact and executable skill retrieval at scale\footnote{Code is available at: https://github.com/ZhiyuanLi218/Caskg }.

摘要：可重用的技能庫允許大型語言模型（LLM）代理在任務之間重用程序知識，但它們也將記憶訪問轉變為一個具有挑戰性的檢索問題。完整庫提示在高上下文成本下保持覆蓋，向量檢索返回緊湊的鄰域，但將技能視為獨立文本，而基於圖形的檢索僅在承載相關性的邊緣可靠時才能恢復工作流程上下文。我們提出了CaSKG，一個反事實因果技能圖框架，在檢索之前校準程序關係。CaSKG首先從語義、詞彙、輸入/輸出和結構證據中構建一個高召回率的有向候選圖，修復證據和可選的LLM評判進一步細化候選分數。然後，它應用方向條件的文本反事實探針，移除、替換和重新排序技能對，使用貝葉斯平滑聚合證據，並發布一個狀態過濾的加權圖以進行任務條件擴展。該圖是在離線構建的，並在不改變下游代理政策或任務介面的情況下使用。在ALFWorld ID-140和ScienceWorld U211的六個LLM骨幹中，CaSKG在所有十二種模型和基準的組合中達到了最高的任務分數。相對於技能圖（GoS），它將六模型的宏平均ScienceWorld分數從72.62提高到80.50，ALFWorld的成功率從80.01\%提高到86.79\%，同時減少了兩個基準的平均環境步驟。定性和消融分析進一步顯示，校準的邊緣有助於檢索保持前提、狀態變更動作、驗證例程和最終完成步驟。這些結果將邊緣信心校準定位為在規模上實現緊湊和可執行技能檢索的有效途徑\footnote{代碼可在：https://github.com/ZhiyuanLi218/Caskg }。

##### **A Storage-Retrieval Gap in Parametric Knowledge Graph Memory**
2608.25489v1 by Martino M. L. Pulici, Cuong Xuan Chu, Evgeny Kharlamov, Volker Tresp

Graph retrieval-augmented generation places retrieved subgraphs into the model's context window at query time, paying a recurring token cost and exposing source data on every call. We study an alternative: compiling a knowledge graph offline into a bank of LoRA adapters, one per entity, that serve as a parametric knowledge layer queried by injecting weights rather than text, at zero query-time context cost. On the MetaQA dataset, we find that subgraph-trained adapters encode context-free factual knowledge that generalizes to unseen questions: on single-valued relations the adapter gains $+0.243$ exact-match score over a base model that is nearly blind closed-book ($0.007$), and only the correct adapter recovers this knowledge (an oracle gap of $+0.283$ over the base model). However, the stored knowledge is not recoverable by similarity: given a query with no subgraph, embedding-based and weight-space geometry retrieval both perform at chance, because a semantically neighbouring entity's adapter does not contain the answer - knowledge is stored locally and does not transfer. Weight geometry correlates with subgraph semantics ($ρ= +0.329$) but not with functional retrievability. We quantify the byte and context-token costs against graph retrieval-augmented generation and discuss deployment implications. Our results establish that parametric knowledge graph memory is feasible for storing knowledge, and identify selecting and composing the right adapters by a mechanism other than semantic similarity as the central open problem - motivating a learned, query-conditioned composition mechanism.

摘要：圖形檢索增強生成在查詢時將檢索到的子圖放入模型的上下文窗口，支付重複的標記成本並在每次調用時暴露源數據。我們研究了一種替代方案：將知識圖譜離線編譯成一個 LoRA 適配器庫，每個實體一個，作為一個參數化知識層，通過注入權重而非文本進行查詢，查詢時上下文成本為零。在 MetaQA 數據集上，我們發現子圖訓練的適配器編碼了無上下文的事實知識，這些知識可以推廣到未見過的問題：在單值關係上，適配器的精確匹配得分比幾乎盲目的基礎模型（$0.007$）提高了 $+0.243$，而且只有正確的適配器能夠恢復這些知識（相對於基礎模型的 oracle 差距為 $+0.283$）。然而，存儲的知識無法通過相似性恢復：給定一個沒有子圖的查詢，基於嵌入和權重空間幾何的檢索表現均為隨機，因為語義相鄰實體的適配器不包含答案——知識是局部存儲的，並不會轉移。權重幾何與子圖語義相關（$ρ= +0.329$），但與功能可檢索性無關。我們量化了與圖形檢索增強生成相比的字節和上下文標記成本，並討論了部署的影響。我們的結果確立了參數化知識圖譜記憶在存儲知識方面的可行性，並確定通過非語義相似性的機制選擇和組合正確的適配器是中心的開放問題——激勵了一種學習的、查詢條件的組合機制。

##### **ReliableRAG: Combating Misinformation in Retrieval-Augmented Generation via Reliability-Guided Reasoning Chains**
2608.25487v1 by Jinpu Jiang, Xuan Wu, Wenhao Song, Bo Yang, You Zhou, Hongwei Ge, Heow Pueh Lee, Yanchun Liang, Chunguo Wu

Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has emerged as a powerful architecture for Question Answering (QA) by integrating external information into Large Language Models (LLMs). However, false, inaccurate, and misleading information in news and social media poses a serious challenge to real-world RAG systems, especially in multi-hop QA, where complex multi-step reasoning can be misled by even a single deceptive misinformation segment in the retrieved documents. Existing approaches mainly rely on implicit alignment or explicit regulation, but their limited ability to assess fine-grained information reliability makes them vulnerable to deceptive misinformation that is semantically relevant to the question yet factually incorrect, leading to erroneous answers. To address this limitation, we propose ReliableRAG, which, to the best of our knowledge, is the first reliability-driven framework that mitigates deceptive misinformation in multi-hop QA through fine-grained evaluation of individual triples. ReliableRAG first extracts information segments from source documents and represents them as structured triples. It then quantifies triple reliability by combining query-triple semantic relevance with triple credibility, retaining only the top-$K$ reliable and non-redundant triples. Based on these refined triples, ReliableRAG autoregressively constructs robust reasoning chains to consolidate trustworthy evidence and filter deceptive misinformation, producing accurate answers faithful to reliable information. Experiments on three multi-hop QA datasets show that ReliableRAG outperforms existing methods, substantially improving the factual reliability and robustness of RAG systems under deceptive misinformation injection.

摘要：檢索增強生成（RAG）已成為一種強大的問題回答（QA）架構，通過將外部信息整合到大型語言模型（LLMs）中。然而，新聞和社交媒體中的虛假、不準確和誤導性信息對現實世界的 RAG 系統構成了嚴重挑戰，特別是在多跳 QA 中，複雜的多步推理甚至可能因檢索文檔中的單一誤導性錯誤信息而受到誤導。現有的方法主要依賴於隱式對齊或顯式規範，但它們在評估細粒度信息可靠性方面的能力有限，使其容易受到與問題語義相關但事實上不正確的誤導性錯誤信息的影響，導致錯誤的答案。為了解決這一限制，我們提出了 ReliableRAG，據我們所知，這是第一個基於可靠性的框架，通過對個別三元組的細粒度評估來減輕多跳 QA 中的誤導性錯誤信息。ReliableRAG 首先從源文檔中提取信息片段，並將其表示為結構化的三元組。然後，它通過將查詢-三元組語義相關性與三元組可信度相結合來量化三元組的可靠性，只保留前 $K$ 個可靠且不冗餘的三元組。基於這些精煉的三元組，ReliableRAG 自回歸地構建穩健的推理鏈，以整合可信的證據並過濾誤導性錯誤信息，生成忠於可靠信息的準確答案。在三個多跳 QA 數據集上的實驗顯示，ReliableRAG 超越了現有方法，顯著提高了 RAG 系統在誤導性錯誤信息注入下的事實可靠性和穩健性。

#### Abstracts
##### **Aero Hand Open: A Simulation-Ready Tendon-Driven Hand for Dexterous Manipulation Learning**
2608.28578v1 by Nan Wang, Mohit Yadav, Jonathan Wulff, Aidan Rosenbaum, Kezhou Chen, Yuvan Sharma, Xu Dong, Yiwei Tao

Tendon-driven hands are anthropomorphic, and moving the actuators off the joints is what makes a hand of this capability affordable to build. Two effects produce that saving. Routing force through a cable removes the requirement that a motor fit inside the joint it drives, so smaller and cheaper motors suffice, and one motor can drive several joints through a single cable, so fewer motors are needed. They are also harder to learn on than a direct-drive hand. The underactuated transmission that produces the saving is itself difficult to represent in a simulator, and the joints one cable drives are not independently commandable. We present Aero Hand Open, a tendon-driven anthropomorphic hand that is released simulation-ready. Three things ship with it. A simulation model reproduces the cable transmission itself. An identified actuation map connects that model to the motor commands in both directions, including the three-way coupling of the thumb. A reinforcement learning package trains policies for the hand. Together they let a policy be trained entirely in simulation and run on the hand with no fine-tuning and no state estimation. We release the mechanical design, the simulation model, the identified mapping, the training environment and the deployment stack.

摘要：肌腱驅動的手是類人型的，將驅動器移出關節使得這種能力的手變得可負擔建造。這種節省是由兩個效應產生的。通過電纜傳遞力量消除了馬達必須安裝在其驅動的關節內的要求，因此可以使用更小且更便宜的馬達，並且一個馬達可以通過一根電纜驅動多個關節，因此需要的馬達更少。它們也比直接驅動的手更難學習。產生節省的欠驅動傳動系統本身在模擬器中難以表示，而一根電纜驅動的關節也無法獨立控制。我們推出了Aero Hand Open，一款已經準備好進行模擬的肌腱驅動類人手。隨之而來的有三樣東西。一個模擬模型再現了電纜傳動系統本身。一個識別的驅動映射將該模型與馬達命令雙向連接，包括拇指的三向耦合。一個強化學習包訓練手的策略。這三者一起使得策略可以完全在模擬中訓練，並在手上運行，無需微調和狀態估計。我們發布了機械設計、模擬模型、識別映射、訓練環境和部署堆棧。

##### **Learning a Size-Weight Frontier for Synthetic-Augmented Inference**
2608.28576v1 by Chengpiao Huang, Kaizheng Wang

Synthetic data can improve statistical inference when real data are scarce, but naively treating synthetic samples as real data can introduce bias and lead to unreliable inference. We develop a general framework for synthetic-augmented inference across a population of related tasks. It characterizes synthetic augmentation by the number of synthetic observations and their weight. Central to our framework is a size-weight frontier that specifies, for each weight, the largest synthetic sample size for which all smaller sizes attain the target task-marginal coverage. We estimate this frontier from historical tasks, and establish a finite-sample coverage guarantee simultaneously for all size-weight configurations on or below the estimated frontier. In experiments using large language model responses to augment opinion survey data, our procedure achieves target coverage and substantially narrows confidence intervals.

摘要：合成數據可以在真實數據稀缺時改善統計推斷，但天真地將合成樣本視為真實數據可能會引入偏差並導致不可靠的推斷。我們開發了一個通用框架，用於在相關任務的整個群體中進行合成增強推斷。它通過合成觀察數量及其權重來表徵合成增強。我們框架的核心是一個大小-權重邊界，它為每個權重指定了最大的合成樣本大小，對於所有較小的大小，均可達到目標任務邊際覆蓋。我們從歷史任務中估計這個邊界，並同時為所有在估計邊界上或以下的大小-權重配置建立有限樣本覆蓋保證。在使用大型語言模型的回應來增強意見調查數據的實驗中，我們的程序達到了目標覆蓋並大幅縮小了置信區間。

##### **A Formal Limitation on Learning Human Language From Textual Corpora**
2608.28560v1 by Emily Cheng, Ryan Cotterell

Can a listener recover what a speaker means from the form of an utterance alone? We answer this question information-theoretically, and for a listener given by any featurizer of text, including the hidden states of contemporary large language models. Modeling language use as a joint distribution over meanings, contexts, and utterances, we derive upper bounds on the probability that a decoder recovers a speaker's intended meaning from a representation of the utterance. The bounds are governed by the uncertainty that form leaves about meaning, which splits into an irreducible part and a part that only (extralinguistic) context, but never the utterance alone, can resolve. Because these quantities are intrinsic to language, no representation, however much text or supervision produced it, can surpass them; the bounds hold whether the space of meanings is discrete or continuous. Experiments on artificial languages, Mandarin zero-pronoun resolution, and color reference provide empirical evidence in support of the theory.

摘要：聽者能否僅根據話語的形式來理解說話者的意思？我們從信息理論的角度回答這個問題，並考慮任何文本特徵提取器所給出的聽者，包括當代大型語言模型的隱藏狀態。將語言使用建模為意義、上下文和話語的聯合分佈，我們推導出解碼器從話語的表示中恢復說話者意圖的意義的概率上限。這些上限由形式對意義所留下的不確定性所主導，這種不確定性分為不可簡約部分和僅能由（超語言）上下文解決的部分，但單靠話語無法解決。因為這些量是語言的內在特性，無論生成了多少文本或監督，任何表示都無法超越它們；這些上限在意義的空間是離散還是連續的情況下都成立。對人工語言、普通話零代詞解析和顏色參照的實驗提供了支持該理論的實證證據。

##### **Blog: Survey of Optimizers**
2608.28557v1 by Ruoran Xu

Neural-network optimization in 2025-2026 is no longer well described as a succession of new Adam variants. The design space has expanded from coordinates to matrices and layers, from fixed training horizons to policies over time, and from mathematical update rules to state representations that must survive sharding and low-precision computation. This survey organizes recent optimizers and training optimization methods along four largely independent axes: temporal estimation, update geometry, horizon management, and representation and systems. It connects the spectral normalization of Muon, the historical matrix statistics of Shampoo and SOAP, adaptive and hybrid matrix methods, memory-efficient optimizers, schedule-free training, small-batch corrections, and quantized optimizer states. The central empirical conclusion is deliberately non-triumphal: matrix-aware methods represent a genuine advance, but there is no context-independent replacement for AdamW. Rankings change with model scale, data-to-parameter ratio, batch size, schedule, parameter partition, tuning budget, and whether the target metric is tokens, FLOPs, wall-clock time, or memory. The practical consequence is a compositional view of optimizer design and a stricter protocol for evaluating optimizer claims.

摘要：神經網絡優化在2025-2026年不再僅僅被描述為一系列新的Adam變體。設計空間已經從坐標擴展到矩陣和層，從固定的訓練範圍擴展到隨時間變化的策略，並且從數學更新規則擴展到必須在分片和低精度計算中生存的狀態表示。這項調查沿著四個大致獨立的軸組織了最近的優化器和訓練優化方法：時間估計、更新幾何、範圍管理，以及表示和系統。它將Muon的光譜正則化、Shampoo和SOAP的歷史矩陣統計、自適應和混合矩陣方法、內存高效的優化器、無排程訓練、小批量修正和量化優化器狀態聯繫在一起。中心的實證結論故意不帶勝利的語氣：矩陣感知方法代表了一種真正的進步，但沒有上下文獨立的AdamW替代品。排名隨著模型規模、數據與參數比率、批量大小、排程、參數劃分、調整預算，以及目標指標是令牌、FLOPs、牆鐘時間還是內存而變化。實際的後果是對優化器設計的組合視角，以及對評估優化器聲明的更嚴格協議。

##### **Logos: An Agent Harness on a Cross-Process Bus**
2608.28553v1 by Hanzhang Jia, Liheng Zeng, Hao Cheng, Yi Gao, Bo Ma

Modern agent systems assemble capabilities at runtime, and this dynamic composition has recently received a complete formal treat ment in the spatiotemporal-composability calculus, in which a capability is a component carrying a tracked inverse, and agents are assembled as plugins. This plugin form is carried by a single process sharing one context, a carrier that places all components in one physical failure domain, a fault suspends every component at once, and process death interrupts every session the process hosts. This paper shows that neither the modeling nor the calculus binds an agent to one process, the statelessness of the language model keeps all cross-step state outside the model, and the soundness invariant is defined on the state space alone. These observations condense into four lemmas whose premises are the hypotheses of the calculus and the statelessness of language-model inference. On these lemmas this paper constructs Logos, a ROS-like cross process agent harness in which a plugin is a process and the only shared state is an append-only transcript. Eighty sessions resume with no repeated effect after kills placed at the four boundaries of the tool-call cycle, and a same-fault comparison with a single process reference configuration shows one fault interrupting every co-resident session while under the peer-process construction one fault ends at one node.

摘要：現代代理系統在運行時組合能力，這種動態組合最近在時空可組合性計算中得到了完整的形式處理，其中能力是一個攜帶跟踪逆的組件，而代理則作為插件組裝。這種插件形式由一個共享一個上下文的單一過程承載，這個承載者將所有組件放置在一個物理故障域中，一個故障會同時暫停每個組件，而過程的死亡會中斷過程所主持的每個會話。本文顯示，無論是建模還是計算都不將代理綁定到一個過程，語言模型的無狀態性將所有跨步狀態保持在模型之外，而健全性不變式僅在狀態空間上定義。這些觀察濃縮成四個引理，其前提是計算的假設和語言模型推理的無狀態性。基於這些引理，本文構建了Logos，一種類似ROS的跨過程代理架構，其中插件是一個過程，唯一共享的狀態是一個僅附加的記錄。在工具調用循環的四個邊界處進行的殺死操作後，八十個會話重新開始，沒有重複的效果，而與單一過程參考配置的同故障比較顯示，一個故障中斷了每個共存的會話，而在對等過程構造下，一個故障僅在一個節點結束。

##### **Video Generative Models as Geometry Learner**
2608.28549v1 by Haosen Yang, Jifei Song, Zhensong Zhang, Xiatian Zhu, Jiankang Deng

Recent generative approaches to geometry estimation adapt pretrained image diffusion models and treat the task as image-conditioned generation. Leveraging off-the-shelf image diffusion models, they either (i) train task-specific geometry models (for depth and surface normal estimation) independently, losing the opportunity of exploring the intrinsic correlation of these geometric targets, or (ii) jointly fine-tune modified image diffusion backbones (e.g., altered self-attention), which typically demands substantial labeled data. To overcome these limitations in a principled fashion, we repurpose pretrained video generative models as a unified and data-efficient framework for geometry estimation, formulated innovatively as a next-frames prediction task. Our method, GeoNeXt, inherits naturally structured knowledge and richer priors from the video model, while further adapting them for joint modeling of images and geometry targets (image <-> geometry), enabling more data efficient and effective learning of geometry. Extensive experiments validate our method for zero-shot monocular depth and surface normal estimation across diverse datasets, outperforming both previous task-specific and unified generative competitors while using substantially less training data. Notably, our method rivals discriminative state-of-the-art approaches trained on over 100x more data and even standouts on several benchmarks.

摘要：最近的幾何估計生成方法適應了預訓練的圖像擴散模型，並將任務視為圖像條件生成。利用現成的圖像擴散模型，它們要麼 (i) 獨立訓練特定任務的幾何模型（用於深度和表面法線估計），失去了探索這些幾何目標內在相關性的機會，要麼 (ii) 聯合微調修改過的圖像擴散骨幹（例如，改變的自注意力），這通常需要大量標記數據。為了以原則性的方式克服這些限制，我們將預訓練的視頻生成模型重新用作幾何估計的統一且數據高效的框架，並創新性地將其表述為下一幀預測任務。我們的方法 GeoNeXt 自然繼承了視頻模型中的結構化知識和更豐富的先驗，同時進一步調整它們以聯合建模圖像和幾何目標（圖像 &lt;-&gt; 幾何），使幾何的學習更加數據高效和有效。大量實驗驗證了我們的方法在多樣數據集上的零樣本單眼深度和表面法線估計中表現優異，超越了之前的特定任務和統一生成競爭者，同時使用的訓練數據顯著更少。值得注意的是，我們的方法與在超過 100 倍數據上訓練的判別最先進方法相抗衡，並在幾個基準測試中脫穎而出。

##### **An Enclosed Mode Is a Gauge Choice: Topology Relative to Reach in Certified Code World Models**
2608.28541v1 by Javier Aguilar Martín

A code world model accepted by a sampling gate can be exactly right on everything the gate can see and arbitrarily wrong beyond it. We characterize what a certified model can know, and what its errors can cost, when the omission is an annular freeze mode enclosing an unreachable interior. The gate quotient makes the question precise: acceptance-with-certainty determines the model exactly on the reachable query set; beyond reach is gauge. On a minimal ring instrument we prove the extreme case (a wrong-topology filled-disc artifact unfalsifiable by any sampling gate and bitwise harmless at play) and measure, with LLM synthesis across three model families, how one knob (a channel of width gamma) walks the same artifact through three regimes: unfalsifiable-and-harmless, falsifiable-and-costly, and instantly falsified. Three principles organize the empirics. First, danger is topology relative to reach: a channel the planner can use collapses the blind model's exploitation (play cost 1.09 to ~0 over a knee at gamma ~ 0.1), while a hidden channel with the same first Betti number keeps it at full strength (1.12). Second, repair is parameter-bound and sensor-bound: no family recovers the region from outside evidence; from inside, models pose the right topology but cannot pin its parameters, and the posed topology tracks the guiding persistent-homology summary's wrong beta_1 (a sensor with a measured geometric resolution limit), not the truth. SWA requires no post-training, is extremely fast, and requires low memory; therefore, making it an extremely cheap and reliable solution.

摘要：軌跡級的信用分配可以僅使用可驗證的信號來定位工具使用 LLM 代理的哪個模塊導致失敗。我們詢問這種失敗的信用是否應該路由固定的零階/進化策略 (ZO/ES) 擾動預算。在一個合成環境中，以及凍結的 Qwen2.5-1.5B/3B 和 SmolLM2-1.7B 代理中，三個任務系列、六種分配方案、一個信用噪聲掃描、配對種子和精確的符號翻轉測試，我們發現在任何池內比較中，對均勻分配沒有統計上可檢測的改進（至少沒有增加 2 個百分點）。聯合 soft-plus-sigma 方案在 1.5B 和 3B 上在 +/- 0.02 AUC 的範圍內等同於均勻；將全部預算集中在信用 argmax 上在 1.5B 上邊際等同，因為該模塊是經過驗證的瓶頸，而在 3B 上則顯著更差。逆傾向去偏差並未挽救路由，錯誤路由在內部成本高達 -0.074 AUC，並在 BFCL 衍生的系列中端到端成本為 -0.118。在六個固定步驟計畫中，損失與瓶頸飢餓率成線性關係 (R^2 = 0.94，描述性)，並且預註冊的無信用覆蓋底線消除了檢測到的傷害。匹配預算的突發和步驟補償追趕計畫與由於累積參數移動不足而產生的傷害是一致的，而不是更新頻率。我們的主要估計量是固定任務池上的優化效率。在未見的 BFCL 函數上，這項研究的唯一例外是，soft routing 在保留的端點上超過均勻 (+0.047，p = 0.031，n = 6)。一種合理但未經測試的解讀是，路由偏好的呼叫者改進會轉移，而均勻的池內增益反映了我們的裝置特有的合成器行為。我們明確報告這一例外並記錄三種失敗模式，這些模式可能會無聲地使凍結的 LLM 上的 ZO/ES 實驗失效。

##### **When Can Conditional Flow Matching Replace Pointwise Negative Log-Likelihood?**
2608.28010v1 by Yansen Han, Hongxin Sun, Tao Lin

Flow matching enables likelihood-free training, yet alignment methods increasingly reuse conditional flow matching (CFM) losses as endpoint negative log-likelihoods (NLLs) and their old/new differences as log-likelihood ratios. We characterize when these substitutions are valid. For linear Gaussian paths, we exactly decompose endpoint NLL into entropy, a weighted CFM objective, an interior velocity--score residual, and a boundary residual. Thus CFM-only estimates and differences are exact only when the corresponding residuals cancel. At the off-policy population optimum, ordinary CFM is not generally a pointwise NLL estimator, whereas \(w_{\mathrm{sc}}(t)=(1-t)/t\) removes the interior residual; this positive result does not extend generally to training or on-policy alignment. On-policy log-ratios can remain biased even for identical endpoint laws or after surrogate optimization. Experiments across dimensions, distributions, and geometries support these conclusions and the mechanisms that make inexact ratios useful. **More broadly, the decomposition provides a theoretical basis for adapting likelihood-based LLM methods to flow matching, while distinguishing exact substitutions from controlled surrogates.**

摘要：流匹配使得無需考慮似然的訓練成為可能，然而對齊方法越來越多地重用條件流匹配（CFM）損失作為端點負對數似然（NLL）以及它們的舊/新差異作為對數似然比。我們界定了這些替代方案何時有效。對於線性高斯路徑，我們精確地將端點 NLL 分解為熵、一個加權的 CFM 目標、一個內部速度-分數殘差和一個邊界殘差。因此，僅使用 CFM 的估計和差異只有在相應的殘差相互抵消時才是精確的。在非政策人口最優解中，普通 CFM 通常不是逐點 NLL 估計器，而 \(w_{\mathrm{sc}}(t)=(1-t)/t\) 刪除了內部殘差；這一正面結果並不普遍適用於訓練或政策對齊。即使對於相同的端點分佈，或在替代優化之後，政策對齊的對數比仍然可能存在偏差。跨維度、分佈和幾何的實驗支持這些結論以及使不精確比率有用的機制。**更廣泛地說，這一分解為將基於似然的 LLM 方法適應於流匹配提供了理論基礎，同時區分精確替代和受控替代。**

##### **Beyond Global Scalars: Synergizing Token-Level Statistics and Deep Semantics for Adversarial AIGC Text Detection**
2608.28009v1 by Peiming Li, Yifan Wang, Zhiyuan Hu, Shiyu Li, Zheng Wei, Yang Tang

The rapid evolution of large language models necessitates robust machine-generated text detection. Existing paradigms typically follow two isolated tracks. Training-free methods rely on global statistical scalars such as perplexity, while training-based methods utilize semantic hidden states. Both approaches exhibit fundamental vulnerabilities in adversarial scenarios. Global scalars act as lossy compressions that obscure local probabilistic burstiness in interleaved texts, whereas pure semantic models overfit to specific fingerprints and remain susceptible to spoofing. To expose these flaws, we introduce MOSAIC, a comprehensive adversarial benchmark comprising 16000 samples across a full-granularity attack spectrum. To address these challenges, we propose NeuroStat, an end-to-end framework bridging the statistical and semantic gap. NeuroStat captures uncompressed token-level probabilistic logits alongside deep semantic hidden states from a single causal language model backbone. We fuse these heterogeneous signals through Macro-State Residual Modulation, which adaptively calibrates local convolutional features using global uncertainty indicators. Orthogonal and contrastive losses further ensure the learning of complementary representations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that NeuroStat maintains exceptional robustness on MOSAIC compared to the severe degradation of state-of-the-art methods, establishing a new standard for adversarial text detection. Code and the MOSAIC benchmark are available at https://github.com/TencentBAC/NeuroStat.

摘要：大型語言模型的快速演變需要強大的機器生成文本檢測。現有的範式通常遵循兩條孤立的路徑。無需訓練的方法依賴於全局統計標量，例如困惑度，而基於訓練的方法則利用語義隱藏狀態。這兩種方法在對抗場景中都顯示出基本的脆弱性。全局標量作為有損壓縮，掩蓋了交錯文本中的局部概率突發性，而純語義模型則過度擬合特定的指紋，並且容易受到欺騙。為了揭示這些缺陷，我們引入了MOSAIC，一個包含16000個樣本的綜合對抗基準，涵蓋了全範圍的攻擊光譜。為了解決這些挑戰，我們提出了NeuroStat，一個端到端的框架，橋接統計和語義之間的差距。NeuroStat捕獲了未壓縮的標記級概率邏輯，同時從單一的因果語言模型主幹中獲取深層語義隱藏狀態。我們通過宏狀態殘差調製融合這些異質信號，該方法根據全局不確定性指標自適應地校準局部卷積特徵。正交和對比損失進一步確保了互補表示的學習。廣泛的實驗表明，與最先進的方法的嚴重降級相比，NeuroStat在MOSAIC上保持了卓越的穩健性，為對抗文本檢測建立了新的標準。代碼和MOSAIC基準可在https://github.com/TencentBAC/NeuroStat上獲得。
